Method: 构建一个由本体论和多个LLM组成的认知架构。每个LLM通过本体论中的领域特定信息进行接地（grounding），并赋予其简单明确的角色分工。该框架能够判断任务对给定UUV的可行性、执行任务规划，并在仿真环境中执行任务，操作员可参与规划和执行过程。

Result: GPT-OSS在可行性评估、任务规划和执行方面表现最佳，而Qwen2.5最适合从自然语言输入中识别任务类型。

Conclusion: 将本体论与LLM结合使用，既能将决策建立在相关领域知识的基础上，又能利用LLM的推理能力，有望提升ROV操作的安全性和操作员的工作效率。

Abstract: Operators remain essential to Remotely Operated Vehicle (ROV) operation, yet often suffer from low situational awareness and high workload, both of which negatively affect safety. This paper presents a cognitive architecture consisting of an ontology and multiple Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist the operator at all stages of the mission. Each LLM is grounded with domain-specific information from the ontology and given a simple role to create a system that can support the operator at all stages of an operation. We are aiming to prove that using the two together will allow decisions to be grounded in the relevant domain knowledge, but also benefit from the reasoning capabilities of the LLM. Our framework determines if a mission is possible for a given Unmanned Underwater Vehicle (UUV), performs mission planning, and executes a given mission in simulation. The operator can be involved in planning and execution, ensuring the resulting plan is valid and that the vehicle behaves safely during execution. We compare different LLMs, Llama3, GPT-OSS, and Qwen2.5, to determine which are best suited to the different roles within our framework. We find that GPT-OSS performs best for feasibility assessment, planning, and execution, while Qwen2.5 is best suited to identifying mission types from natural language input.

</details>


### [286] [Bridging Semantics and Physics with Constrained LLMs for Safe and Trustworthy Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29379)
*Wenhao Hong,Lan Wei,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言引导的机器人操作框架，通过将推理-执行边界形式化为类型化契约，利用MCP协议验证工具调用、MoveIt Task Constructor进行运动规划，并在物理UFactory 850上实现高达80%的倾倒任务和90%的抓取放置任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够将指令分解为动作序列，但存在语言-动作鸿沟：语言上看似正确的计划可能由于运动学和碰撞约束而物理上不可行。需要弥合这一差距，确保计划不仅看起来正确，还能在杂乱环境和感知不完美的条件下安全执行。

Method: 将推理-执行边界形式化为类型化契约；从RGB-D观测中构建基于碰撞感知的显式场景模型；通过MCP（模型上下文协议）定义的schema验证工具调用来约束语言层决策，拒绝畸形命令；每个验证后的调用通过MoveIt Task Constructor流水线进行确定性落地，在验证后行动（verify-then-act）步骤中评估候选运动，仅将通过运动学和碰撞检查的轨迹发送给机器人。

Result: 在物理UFactory 850上，涉及液体、颗粒介质和离散固体的倾倒任务中，每个任务十次试验成功率最高达80%；抓取放置任务使用相同的规划、协议和验证栈达到90%成功率。脚本策略在最简单任务上略优，但在最难任务上成功率降至10%，而本方法达到60%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效地弥合了语言-动作鸿沟，在不依赖脚本预编程的情况下，通过协议验证和物理约束检查实现了可靠的语言引导机器人操作，尤其在复杂任务中显著优于脚本策略。

Abstract: A language-guided robot operating in a real kitchen must do more than produce a plan that appears correct. It must also execute that plan safely in cluttered environments under imperfect perception. Large language models (LLM) can decompose instructions into action sequences, yet a language-action gap remains: a plan may appear valid linguistically while being physically infeasible under kinematic and collision constraints. We bridge this gap by formalizing the reasoning-execution boundary as a typed contract. From RGB-D observations, the system grounds perceived objects in an explicit, collision-aware scene model and constrains language-level decisions through schema-validated tool calls defined by the Model Context Protocol (MCP), rejecting malformed commands before they reach the robot. Each validated call is deterministically grounded in a MoveIt Task Constructor pipeline, where candidate motions are evaluated against the reconstructed planning scene in a verify-then-act step. Only trajectories that pass both kinematic and collision checks are sent to the robot. On a physical UFactory 850, the method achieves up to 80% success across ten trials per task on pouring tasks involving liquids, granular media, and discrete solids. It achieves 90% success on a grasp-and-place task using the same planning, protocol, and verification stack. Although a scripted policy slightly outperforms our method on the easiest task, its success rate falls to 10% on the hardest, compared with 60% for our method.

</details>


### [287] [Toward Trustworthy Robot-Assisted Sliding Palpation for Shallow Vessel Localisation with a Calibrated Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29396)
*Piotr Blaszyk,Wen Fan,Kaizhong Deng,Daniel Elson,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器人辅助滑动触诊框架，利用校准的数字孪生生成带标签的触觉序列数据，训练时空图神经网络进行浅表血管定位，实现了模拟到真实的跨域迁移，定位误差在1.05-5.49mm之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅表血管的可靠定位对机器人辅助静脉穿刺等操作至关重要，但真实硬件上采集触觉数据成本高、耗时且易损坏软视觉触觉传感器，因此需要减少对真实数据依赖的解决方案。

Method: 构建校准的数字孪生模型模拟传感器-血管接触，通过贝叶斯优化域适应实现与真实触诊轨迹的校准，并随机化滑动方向和接触条件。使用时空图神经网络对模拟标记轨迹进行逐节点血管分类，通过2D-3D-2D几何投影生成人类可验证的俯视定位图。

Result: 校准孪生在最深接触处的模拟到真实标记对齐平均绝对误差为0.50mm。重投影到1mm网格后，预测血管像素距真实血管像素平均1.05-5.49mm，除Sim到Meat外其余配置为1.05-1.31mm。Sim到Meat的较大误差反映了更大的域差距。

Conclusion: 该工作通过校准模拟、可解释定位和透明的跨域评估，展示了向可信赖的触觉触诊方向取得的进展，并公开了代码、模型权重和数据。

Abstract: Reliable localisation of shallow subsurface vessels is important for safe robot-assisted venous access and vessel-aware manipulation, but collecting diverse tactile data on physical hardware is costly, time-consuming, and can degrade soft vision-based tactile sensors. We present a robot-assisted sliding-palpation framework in which a calibrated digital twin generates labelled tactile sequences, reducing reliance on real-world data. The twin models sensor-vessel contact, is calibrated against real palpation trajectories using Bayesian-optimisation-based domain adaptation, and is randomised over sliding direction and contact conditions. A spatio-temporal graph neural network trained on simulated marker trajectories performs per-node vessel classification and produces a human-verifiable top-view localisation map through 2D-to-3D-to-2D geometric projection. We evaluate three datasets: Sim, Silicone, and Meat, the latter a raw-meat phantom with vessel models at nominal depths of 0 to 30 mm, using four train-to-test configurations: Sim to Sim, Sim to Silicone, Sim to Meat, and Meat to Silicone. The calibrated twin achieves a simulated-to-real marker-alignment mean absolute error of 0.50 mm at deepest contact across four canonical interactions. After reprojection onto a 1 mm top-view grid, predicted vessel pixels lie on average 1.05 to 5.49 mm from the nearest true vessel pixel across the four models, with 1.05 to 1.31 mm for all except Sim to Meat. The larger error for Sim to Meat reflects the greater domain shift and current limit of simulation transfer. These results demonstrate progress toward trustworthy tactile palpation through calibrated simulation, interpretable localisation, and transparent cross-domain evaluation. Code, model weights, and data are publicly available on GitHub and Zenodo.

</details>


### [288] [SMILE: Smooth Motion for Improved Long-Horizon VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29432)
*Jongwoo Park,E-Ro Nguyen,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Cristina Mata,Xiang Li,Michael S Ryoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SMILE，一种仅改变动作表示（预测B样条系数并解码为平滑动作序列）的架构无关接口，可在不改变基线模型骨架和规模的情况下实现更长固定水平的动作生成，在多个VLA模型和基准上同时提升准确率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通过每次调用执行多个动作来降低推理成本，但更长的动作水平往往导致精度下降，因为原始动作块包含抖动和离群值。现有方法在延长水平时面临准确性与效率之间的权衡。

Method: SMILE是一种架构保留接口，预测B样条系数并将解码为平滑动作序列。它仅改变动作表示层，不修改骨干网络和模型规模，从而支持更长的固定水平。应用于SmolVLA、Evo1、VPP和DAWN四个基线模型。

Result: SMILE-Evo1在LIBERO上达到98.0%准确率并带来1.1倍加速；SMILE-VPP在CALVIN上平均任务长度4.42并实现1.5倍加速；在匹配10步执行水平下，SMILE-SmolVLA将非边界加速度降低78.6%、速度符号变化率降低42.3%；真实xArm实验显示更高成功率、更少掉落和更少碰撞。

Conclusion: 平滑系数空间生成是通往准确、高效长水平VLA执行的有效途径，确立了SMILE作为一种通用且有效的动作表示改进方法的地位。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models reduce inference cost by executing multiple actions per call, but longer horizons often degrade accuracy because raw chunks contain jitter and outliers. We introduce SMILE, an architecture-preserving interface that predicts B-spline coefficients and decodes them into smooth action sequences. SMILE changes only the action representation, enabling longer fixed horizons while retaining each baseline's backbone and model scale. We apply SMILE to SmolVLA, Evo1, VPP, and DAWN, improving accuracy and amortized inference efficiency across LIBERO, CALVIN, and real-world experiments. SMILE-Evo1 reaches 98.0% with a 1.1x speedup on LIBERO, while SMILE-VPP reaches an average length of 4.42 with a 1.5x speedup on CALVIN. At a matched execution horizon of 10, SMILE-SmolVLA reduces non-boundary acceleration by 78.6% and velocity sign-change rate by 42.3%. Real-world xArm tests show higher success, fewer drops, and fewer contacts. These results establish smooth coefficient-space generation as a route to accurate, efficient long-horizon VLA execution. Project page: jongwoopark7978.github.io/smilevla

</details>


### [289] [Blind Dexterity: Whole-Body Humanoid Manipulation via Pure Proprioception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29487)
*Aditya Bhatt,Oleg Kaidanov,Puze Liu,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了在Unitree G1人形机器人上仅依靠本体感觉（关节编码器）实现的全身操纵技能，无需摄像头、标记、力觉或触觉传感器。作者提出关节编码器信号在柔性接触下形成全身触觉通道，通过训练策略主动探测环境来解码物体状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全身操纵依赖多模态传感器（相机、力传感器、触觉等），但作者质疑这一前提，探索仅利用商业化机器人已广泛配置的关节编码器是否足以完成复杂全身操纵任务。

Method: 在Unitree G1上训练全身控制策略，仅使用关节编码器读数。通过生成接触丰富的运动让策略主动探测环境，并联合训练独立的紧凑任务特定状态估计器，从短时本体感觉历史中解码物体状态。

Result: 策略在五个定性不同的任务上表现出色：无IMU的抗推双足行走、足球停球、寻找并提拉行李箱把手、安装随机位置的滑板。状态估计器的预测误差在信息性接触后迅速下降。

Conclusion: 基于关节编码器的本体感觉结合柔性驱动已足以作为全身灵巧操纵和交互感知的实用基础，可在此基础上叠加更丰富的传感模态。

Abstract: We present blind, whole-body manipulation skills on a Unitree G1 humanoid using only onboard proprioception, without cameras, markers, force-torque, or tactile sensors. Despite this minimal sensing, the trained policies exhibit surprising capability across qualitatively different tasks: push-resilient bipedal walking without IMU feedback, active soccer ball trapping with a foot, seeking and lifting a suitcase by its handle, and mounting a randomly positioned skateboard.
  We argue that these capabilities arise from a key underappreciated signal: the way the joint encoder readouts evolve under purposeful compliant contact, effectively forming a whole-body tactile channel. By generating contact-rich motions, the trained policies actively probe the environment; as a result, task-relevant object state (e.g., pose) becomes increasingly decodable from short proprioceptive histories. We expose this information using compact task-specific state estimators trained alongside, but fully separately from, the policies; their prediction errors decrease rapidly after informative contact.
  Our results indicate that joint encoder-based proprioception, combined with compliant actuation (now widely available on commercial robots and low-cost motors) is already a strong, practical substrate for whole-body dexterous manipulation and interactive perception, and therefore a natural foundation on which richer sensing can be layered.

</details>


### [290] [A Sliding Window Filter on the Galilean Group for Consistent Aided Inertial Navigation with Unknown Measurement Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29514)
*Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究辅助惯性导航系统中辅助传感器测量存在未知恒定延迟时的联合延迟与导航状态估计问题，利用特殊伽利略群建模，并提出滑动窗口滤波器以改善估计的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助传感器测量延迟会导致导航轨迹修正时序错误，影响导航精度；现有方法未充分处理延迟与状态联合估计的可观测性问题，需要更严格的数学框架与滤波方案。

Method: 在特殊伽利略群上构建状态空间模型，分析延迟与状态联合估计的可观测性，推导单次延迟测量的对称性及测量雅可比零方向导致的信息泄漏问题；提出滑动窗口滤波器，保留短时间导航状态历史并跨活动窗口联合应用延迟辅助校正。

Result: 仿真研究表明：不维持足够窗口的估计器会迅速变得高度不一致，而即使较短的滑动窗口也能显著改善一致性，为可观测性提供所需的时序支撑。

Conclusion: 多时间点联合处理测量对于消除延迟估计中的不可观测方向至关重要，滑动窗口滤波是兼顾计算效率与估计一致性的有效方案。

Abstract: We study aided inertial navigation when the aiding sensor measurements are subject to an unknown constant delay. The goal is to estimate the delay and navigation state jointly so that delayed measurements correct the trajectory at the appropriate times, yielding a more accurate navigation solution. We formulate the problem on the special Galilean group, which provides a natural state-space structure for aided navigation with uncertainty in both motion and timing. We then examine the observability of joint delay and state estimation and show that, for a single delayed measurement, the model admits an exact symmetry in which a change in the delay can be compensated by a change in the navigation state, leaving the measurement unchanged. Processing measurements individually allows spurious information to `leak' along the corresponding null direction of the measurement Jacobian, producing overconfident and inconsistent estimates. Applying measurements from multiple times together can eliminate this direction when the trajectory is informative enough. Motivated by this result, we develop a sliding window filter that retains a short history of navigation states and applies delayed aiding corrections jointly across the active window. We conduct a series of simulation studies to characterize estimator accuracy and consistency. The simulations demonstrate that an estimator that does not maintain an adequate window can rapidly become highly inconsistent, whereas even a short sliding window markedly improves consistency by providing the temporal support that observability requires.

</details>


### [291] [Task-Relevant Feature-Dynamics Fidelity Enables Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer for Robotic Ultrasound Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29516)
*Yizhao Qian,Jiayuan Luo,Wanyi Zhu,Yameng Zhang,Max Q. -H. Meng,Yixuan Yuan,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人超声任务的仿真器设计方法，强调"任务相关特征动态保真度"(TR-FDF)对零样本迁移的重要性，并通过理论分析和实验验证了该指标的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声策略在B模式图像上训练需要大量真实交互数据，而真实机器人数据采集成本高且有安全约束。仿真虽可扩展，但零样本迁移不仅取决于单帧真实感，还取决于仿真观测能否复现由探头运动引起的任务相关特征变化，这一跨域一致性问题此前未被系统研究。

Method: 首先通过收缩分析（contraction analysis）证明TR-FDF失配的敏感性会减小有效闭环收缩裕度，而运动无关的误差主要扩大残差误差界。基于此分析，开发了TR-FDF导向的超声仿真器，结合共享结构中间域、轨迹级固定噪声和少步条件流生成技术。

Result: 在体模实验中，纯仿真训练的策略在四个目标平面上390/400次零样本部署成功。仿真器FID为29.66，生成速度达67.1Hz。受控干预、消融和基线对比实验表明，TR-FDF敏感性可补充单帧真实感，共同预测零样本迁移性能。

Conclusion: TR-FDF是比单帧真实感更能捕捉零样本迁移能力的关键指标，所提出的TR-FDF导向仿真器有效弥合了仿真与真实之间的动态一致性差距，为机器人超声策略的仿真训练提供了新的设计准则。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound policies operating directly on B-mode images require extensive interaction data, whereas real-robot data collection is costly and safety-constrained. Simulation provides a scalable alternative, but zero-shot transfer depends not only on single-frame realism but also on whether simulated observations reproduce task-relevant feature changes induced by probe motion. We term this cross-domain consistency task-relevant feature-dynamics fidelity (TR-FDF). Under local regularity assumptions, our contraction analysis shows that greater sensitivity of TR-FDF mismatch to probe motion reduces the effective closed-loop contraction margin, whereas motion-independent errors primarily enlarge the residual error bound. Guided by this analysis, we develop a TR-FDF-oriented ultrasound simulator that combines a shared structural intermediate domain, trajectory-level fixed noise, and few-step conditional flow generation. In phantom experiments, a policy trained exclusively in simulation succeeded in 390 of 400 zero-shot deployments across four target planes. The simulator achieved an FID of 29.66 and generated observations at 67.1 Hz. Controlled interventions, ablations, and baseline comparisons showed that TR-FDF sensitivity complements single-frame realism in predicting zero-shot transfer performance.

</details>


### [292] [AGM: Achievement-Grounded Memory for Closed-Loop Agents with Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29537)
*Hongbo Gao,Zeyu Ni,Xin Wen,Siyu Xu,Ruifeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Achievement-Grounded Memory (AGM)框架，通过子目标序列+进度指针的方式，将冻结的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略从开环执行转变为闭环执行-验证-推进模式，仅在物理证据确证子目标达成后才推进进度，显著提升了多步骤操作任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结VLA策略擅长广泛的操作技能，但以开环动作块执行，无法跟踪任务进度，难以可靠判断继续、重试或终止。外部记忆虽可补救，但将未实际完成的动作误记为已完成会引入持久性任务状态错误。

Method: 将任务建模为子目标序列+进度指针；利用本体感觉交互线索判断何时验证；通过仅2.43M参数的验证头，结合协同点追踪和语言条件跨视角比较（来自冻结基础模型），判断哪些子目标已实现。

Result: 在RoboMME Counting基准上，AGM在PickXTimes和BinFill任务上达到最高性能，平均超越最强记忆增强基线；在物理机器人上也取得同等显著提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的具身记忆更多取决于纪律性的状态更新（何时及如何推进进度），而非记忆容量本身。AGM在不进行测试时大模型推理、保持策略冻结的前提下，实现了闭环控制。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer broad manipulation skills but execute open-loop action chunks without tracking task progress, so the agent cannot reliably decide whether to continue, retry, or terminate. External memory is a natural remedy, yet it can be harmful when attempted actions are treated as completed progress, turning local execution errors into persistent task-state errors. We propose Achievement-Grounded Memory (AGM), a lightweight closed-loop framework for frozen VLA policies that represents a task as a subgoal sequence with a progress pointer and advances this memory only after the current subgoal is verified by physical evidence. Proprioceptive interaction cues decide when to verify, while coherent point tracking and language-conditioned cross-view comparison, sourced from frozen foundation models through a single 2.43M-parameter verification head, decide what was achieved. AGM thereby converts open-loop execution into a closed loop of execution, verification, and progress, keeping the policy frozen without test-time large-model inference. On the RoboMME Counting benchmark, AGM reaches on PickXTimes and on BinFill, surpassing the strongest memory-augmented baseline by points on average, and the framework yields equally decisive gains on a physical robot. Reliable embodied memory thus depends more on disciplined state updates than on memory capacity.

</details>


### [293] [Module Number Adaptive Visual Shape Control for Serial Modular Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29547)
*Kyohei Akamine,Takato Horii,Yusuke Sakaue,Hiroki Ishizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块数量自适应的视觉形状控制方法，用于串联模块化软体气动机器人。仅使用单模块驱动形状数据训练的控制器，通过将整体相机图像分解为局部模块补丁，即可复用于1到5个模块的机器人。实验证明该方法在模块数量变化、环境变化和负载情况下均能实现有效的形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于数据驱动的软体机器人形状控制方法通常针对固定机器人结构设计，当模块数量变化时需要重新收集控制数据，缺乏可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 仅用单模块驱动形状数据训练控制器；2) 通过公共模块分割器将整体相机图像分解为局部模块补丁；3) 对每个补丁应用相同的局部控制器；4) 使用几何数据增强提高对下游模块的可迁移性；5) 利用轻量级掩膜重建网络重建被合成移除的执行器掩膜通道。

Result: 物理机器人实验表明，该方法在1到5个不同模块数量的配置下均能实现形状控制，并在环境变化和负载加载条件下依然有效。

Conclusion: 单模块控制学习能够实现可扩展的整机控制，无需针对特定配置收集控制数据，为模块化软体机器人的自适应控制提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Image based shape control provides a simple means of controlling the whole body configuration of soft robots. However, existing data driven approaches are typically developed for fixed robot structures and require new control data when the number of modules changes. This paper presents a module number adaptive visual shape control method for serial modular soft pneumatic robots. A controller trained only on single module actuation shape data is reused for robots with one to five modules by decomposing whole body camera images into local module patches. A single common module segmenter localizes individual modules across all tested configurations, while the same local controller is applied to every extracted patch. Geometric data augmentation improves transferability to downstream modules, and a lightweight mask reconstruction network reconstructs a synthetically removed actuator mask channel. Experiments on physical robots demonstrate shape control across varying numbers of modules and under environmental changes and payload loading. The results show that single module control learning enables scalable whole body control without configuration specific control data collection.

</details>


### [294] [$\mathcal{N}_0$-Foundation: Towards the Age of Tactile Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29601)
*NeoteAI Team,Fudan TEAI Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了𝒩₀-Foundation，一个触觉驱动的具身操作范式，整合了触觉传感硬件、大规模多模态数据、触觉表示学习和标准化评估。研究团队构建了包含超过30000小时同步视觉-触觉演示的NeoData数据集，并开源了5000小时的OpenNeoData子集，同时提出了跨传感器设计的触觉表示模型NeoForce以及综合基准测试套件（NeoReal和NeoSim），实验表明策略受益于物理接触状态而非设备特定的触觉外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作语料库缺乏大规模触觉数据，而触觉对于可变形物体操作、精密装配、精细力控制和持续表面交互等任务至关重要。作者旨在解决触觉数据规模不足、跨传感器表示不兼容以及缺乏标准化评估的问题。

Method: 1) 工程化构建可扩展数据采集基础设施：视觉触觉传感器、触觉通用操作接口（UMI）和同步视觉-触觉采集系统；2) 构建NeoData数据集（30000+小时，6种具身形态，450个任务）；3) 提出NeoForce视觉-触觉表示模型，学习跨传感器设计的可迁移触觉表示；4) 提出NeoReal和NeoSim双套件标准基准。

Result: 实验表明，利用该基础设施、数据集和触觉表示训练的策略，其性能提升来源于物理接触状态的感知，而非设备特定触觉信号的外观特征。数据集、表示模型和基准已全部公开。

Conclusion: 𝒩₀-Foundation提供了一个完整的触觉具身操作生态系统，涵盖数据收集、表示学习和评估，填补了大规模触觉数据的空白，为触觉驱动的具身操作研究奠定了基础设施，并支持未来在该方向上的持续研究。

Abstract: We present $\mathcal{N}_0$-Foundation, a paradigm for tactile-enabled embodied manipulation, which integrates tactile sensing hardware, large-scale multimodal data, tactile representation learning, and standardized evaluation. First, we engineer the infrastructure for scalable data collection, including a vision-based tactile sensor, a tactile Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), and a synchronized visuo-tactile data collection system supporting both robot embodiments and UMI-based demonstrations. Leveraging this infrastructure, we construct NeoData, which contains more than 30000 hours of synchronized visual and tactile demonstrations, spanning six embodiments, 450 tasks, and billions of paired RGB and tactile frames collected through a mixture of real-robot teleoperation and UMI-based demonstrations. To facilitate open research, we further release OpenNeoData, a 5000-hour open-source subset of NeoData. The dataset addresses a central limitation of existing manipulation corpora, critical for deformable-object manipulation, precise assembly, delicate force control, and sustained surface interaction. Capitalizing on the large-scale, heterogeneous tactile measurements, we propose NeoForce, a visuo-tactile representation model that learn transferable tactile representations across different sensor designs. To enable systematic evaluation of tactile embodied models built upon our infrastructure, datasets and tactile representations, we further propose a comprehensive benchmark, which combines the real-world NeoReal suite and the simulated NeoSim suite for standardized evaluation. Experiments across both suites show that policies benefit from the physical contact state rather than from the device-specific appearance of the tactile signal. We release the dataset, the representation, and the benchmark, aiming at supporting future work on tactile-enabled embodied manipulation.

</details>


### [295] [VeloBins: Learning Velocity and Its Uncertainty via Bins and Error-Conditioned Gaussian Labels for Aerial Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29720)
*Maulana Bisyir Azhari,Seungwook Lee,Donghun Han,Sung Jun Park,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VeloBins方法，将惯性里程计（IO）中的速度回归问题重构为离散速度区间的分类问题，通过区间分布的期望解码速度、方差解码不确定性，避免了额外的不确定性解码器和负对数似然（NLL）损失。在四个空中机器人数据集上，VeloBins相较最强基线将速度、相对轨迹和绝对轨迹误差分别降低3-27%、8-40%和6-53%，且在没有显式优化NLL损失的情况下取得了最低NLL和最佳滤波器一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性里程计对空中机器人至关重要，但现有基于学习的IO方法通过回归学习速度困难，且使用独立的解码器和NLL损失联合估计不确定性会复杂化训练并导致过度自信的估计。

Method: VeloBins将速度回归重构为离散速度区间的分类问题。从区间分布的期望解码速度、从方差解码不确定性，无需单独的不确定性解码器。不确定性监督采用误差条件高斯标签，以真实速度为均值、速度误差为标准差进行显式监督。

Result: 在四个空中数据集（包括自由式激烈飞行、27g纳米四旋翼和时速超21m/s的无人机竞速）上，VeloBins均取得最低平均误差：速度、相对轨迹和绝对轨迹误差分别较最强基线降低3-27%、8-40%和6-53%。即使在未优化NLL损失的情况下，仍实现了最低NLL和最佳滤波器一致性。

Conclusion: 将速度回归转为分类并结合误差条件高斯标签进行不确定性监督，不仅简化了训练流程、消除了对独立不确定性解码器的需求，还显著提升了惯性里程计的精度、不确定度质量和滤波器一致性，验证了该方法在空中机器人多种极端场景下的普遍有效性。

Abstract: Inertial odometry (IO) is critical for aerial robots, where aggressive maneuvers and poor lighting degrade visual sensors. Recent learning-based IO methods improve traditional integration-based approaches by learning motion priors from IMU and platform-specific sensors, then fusing the predictions within an extended Kalman filter. However, learning velocity through regression is difficult, while jointly estimating uncertainty with a separate decoder and negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss further complicates training and can lead to over-confident estimates. We introduce VeloBins, which reformulates velocity regression as classification over discretized velocity bins. We decode both the velocity from the bin distribution's expectation and the uncertainty from its variance, removing the need for a separate uncertainty decoder. We further supervise the uncertainty explicitly using an error-conditioned Gaussian label centered at the ground-truth velocity, with a standard deviation set to the velocity error. We evaluate VeloBins on four aerial datasets, ranging from free-form aggressive flights and a 27 g nano-quadrotor to drone racing at over 21~m/s. VeloBins achieves the lowest average errors on all four datasets, reducing velocity, relative trajectory, and absolute trajectory errors by 3-27%, 8-40%, and 6-53%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Notably, the proposed supervision achieves the lowest NLL and best filter consistency despite never optimizing an NLL loss. The code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [296] [DriftingVLA: Native One-Step Vision-Language-Action Generation via Per-Dimension Temporal Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29749)
*Yuxuan Gao,Shiqi Zhang,Yedong Shen,Yifan Duan,Wenhao Yu,Xin Zhang,Siyuan Cao,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriftingVLA是一种原生单步视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过分布漂移目标直接学习噪声到动作块的映射，避免了传统流式VLA模型多步迭代推理的高延迟问题。引入逐维度时序漂移（PDTD）机制精细建模每个动作维度的分布特性，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人任务上均超越基线，同时实现3.36倍的动作生成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于流的VLA模型虽然能生成表达丰富的连续动作，但依赖多步细化来产生每个动作块，导致在线机器人控制中的高延迟问题。

Method: 提出DriftingVLA，采用分布漂移目标学习直接的噪声到动作块映射，实现单步部署；并提出逐维度时序漂移（PDTD），将每个动作维度的完整时间轨迹视为独立的漂移单元，仅作用于训练目标，共享VLA模型仍联合生成完整动作块以保持跨维度依赖。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.32%成功率，RoboTwin 2.0上81.09%，六个真实单/双臂任务上77.67%，均优于多步流策略和单步VLA基线；动作块生成速度提升3.36倍，无需迭代细化且不牺牲控制性能。

Conclusion: 原生单步部署的DriftingVLA在保持控制性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟，验证了分布漂移目标作为流式VLA替代方案的有效性，为实时机器人控制提供了高效路径。

Abstract: Conventional flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models support expressive continuous action generation but rely on multi-step refinement to produce each action chunk, increasing latency in online robot control. To address this issue, we introduce DriftingVLA, a native one-step VLA that generates a complete action chunk with a single action-expert forward pass. Rather than learning a flow field that requires iterative integration at inference, DriftingVLA uses a distribution-drifting objective to learn a direct noise-to-action-chunk mapping for one-step deployment. Since robot action dimensions carry distinct control semantics and distributional characteristics, we further introduce Per-Dimension Temporal Drifting (PDTD). PDTD treats the complete temporal trajectory of each action dimension as a separate drifting unit, enabling finer-grained modeling and shaping of dimension-specific action distributions. This per-dimension decomposition applies only to the training objective; the shared VLA model still generates the complete action chunk jointly, thereby preserving cross-dimensional dependencies. DriftingVLA achieves 98.32% success on LIBERO, 81.09% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 77.67% across six real-world single- and dual-arm tasks, outperforming the evaluated multi-step flow policy and one-step VLA baselines. Native one-step deployment also delivers a 3.36-fold speedup in action-chunk generation, eliminating iterative refinement without sacrificing control performance.

</details>


### [297] [LARC: Lazy Adaptive Reachability Certification of Robot Manipulator Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29767)
*Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Yuzhe Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种懒惰自适应可达性认证（LARC）方法，用于机器人轨迹的连续时间碰撞检测。该方法仅对无结论的区间进行二分，利用中点胶囊和精确分量级速度最大值约束杆件占用，在保持与固定细粒度基线相同的判定结果下，将区间评估次数减少到24.8%，中位加速比为10.28倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散轨迹检查可能漏检采样状态之间的碰撞，而可达性认证虽能约束状态间的运动，但均匀时间划分在间隙较大区域浪费了计算资源。

Method: LARC通过仅对无结论的清除性测试区间进行二分来检查规划轨迹；对于分段三次Hermite关节轨迹，使用中点胶囊膨胀精确的逐分量速度最大值来约束连杆占用；已认证区间覆盖轨迹，提供连续时间外部障碍物间隙保证。

Result: 在160条AgileX PIPER轨迹（80对起点-终点）上，LARC与深度九的固定细粒度基线判定完全一致，仅使用20328次区间评估（基线工作的24.8%），中位加速比10.28倍。独立MoveIt/FCL审计检查了158051个状态，检测到21个直接插值控制中的碰撞，LARC均未认证这些轨迹。

Conclusion: LARC在共享证书模型下减少了计算量，但139条采样间隙轨迹中仍有27条未获认证；采样审计无法独立证明连续时间的间隙安全性。

Abstract: Discrete trajectory checks can miss collisions between sampled robot states. Reachability-based certification bounds motion between states, but uniform time partitions waste computation where clearance is large. We present lazy adaptive reachability certification (LARC), which checks a planned trajectory by bisecting only intervals with an inconclusive clearance test. For piecewise-cubic Hermite joint trajectories, the method bounds link occupancy using midpoint capsules inflated by exact componentwise speed maxima. Certified intervals covering the trajectory provide continuous-time external-obstacle clearance, subject to geometric containment, static obstacles, and a prescribed margin. On 160 AgileX PIPER trajectories from 80 start-goal pairs, LARC matched all decisions of the fixed-fine baseline at depth nine. It used 20328 interval evaluations (24.8% of baseline work), with a median paired speedup of 10.28x. A separate MoveIt/FCL audit checked 158051 states and detected collisions in 21 direct-interpolation controls, none of which LARC certified. The method reduced computation under a shared certificate model, but 27 of 139 sampled-clear trajectories remained uncertified. The sampled audit cannot independently prove continuous-time clearance.

</details>


### [298] [SmoothRL: Online Reinforcement Learning During Asynchronous Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29768)
*Guang Gao,Yuxuan Nong,Baifu Huang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothRL是一个在线强化学习框架，用于在异步推理循环中微调预训练策略，通过显式建模异步执行过程中的动作块分区来确保梯度优化与真实轨迹分布对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界中部署机器人策略需要同时满足可靠性和实时性，但现有通用模型的推理延迟较高，且异步执行与基于梯度的在线RL集成尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用值梯度范式，直接利用动作值函数对策略动作的梯度更新策略参数；在训练中显式建模异步推理过程，将每个动作块按帧索引分为committed、execution和discarded三个区域，仅通过execution区域传播梯度。

Result: 在需要高精度的真实世界机器人任务以及需要异步执行的高动态任务上进行了评估。

Conclusion: SmoothRL通过将异步执行建模纳入优化过程，实现了在线RL与异步推理的有效集成，为高延迟推理下的实时机器人控制提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying robot policies in the physical world requires satisfying two fundamental desiderata: reliability and smooth real-time execution. However, deploying state-of-the-art generalist models presents challenges on both fronts. Achieving the precision and robustness required for real-world deployment necessitates sample-efficient online reinforcement learning (RL) to adapt pretrained models. Meanwhile, the increasing scale of robot foundation models has led to higher inference latency. To satisfy real-time constraints under high latency, modern systems adopt asynchronous inference with action chunking, overlapping policy computation with chunk execution to hide latency and enable smooth control. Despite their complementary roles, integrating asynchronous execution with gradient-based online RL remains underexplored. We present SmoothRL, an online RL framework that fine-tunes a pretrained policy within an asynchronous inference loop. SmoothRL follows a value-gradient paradigm, directly updating policy parameters using gradients of the action-value function with respect to policy actions. To enable correct optimization under asynchronous execution, SmoothRL explicitly models the asynchronous inference process during training. Specifically, each generated action chunk is partitioned by frame index into three regions: a committed region, consisting of actions committed by the previous inference cycle; an execution region, containing newly generated actions executed by the robot; and a discarded region, containing actions superseded by the next inference cycle. Gradients are propagated only through the execution region, ensuring policy optimization aligns with the trajectory distribution induced by asynchronous execution. We evaluate SmoothRL on real-world robotic tasks requiring high precision, as well as highly dynamic tasks that necessitate asynchronous execution.

</details>


### [299] [Learning Agile Perceptive Traversal of Sparse 3D Structures for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29769)
*Efe Ongan,Chong Zhang,Boyang Sun,Andrei Cramariuc,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的感知控制系统，使轮式人形机器人能够通过头戴式固态激光雷达的原始稀疏点云观测，完成猴架穿越（跳跃上杠、臂行、跳跃下杠）的完整任务序列，在硬件上15次试验中成功14次，臂行速度可达0.5 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏3D结构中的人形机器人导航需要感知细薄悬空几何形状并执行敏捷的全身运动，现有方法难以同时满足感知与控制的精度和速度要求。

Method: 采用基于注意力的编码器结合循环记忆处理原始激光雷达稀疏点云；通过相位调度的教师-学生流水线，组合跳跃上杠、臂行和跳跃下杠的专家策略；在仿真中建模激光雷达噪声、电池电压跌落和执行器热限制；硬件上配备被动钩状末端执行器以增强杠交互鲁棒性。

Result: 硬件实验在三种杠配置下，14/15次试验成功完成跳跃上杠→臂行→跳跃下杠的完整序列，臂行速度最高达0.5 m/s；同一感知骨干还支持单独训练的避障策略，可躲避截面仅2 cm的薄型架空障碍物。

Conclusion: 注意力编码器加循环记忆的感知框架能有效处理稀疏激光雷达数据，且该感知骨干具有良好的可迁移性，支持多种下游运动任务。

Abstract: Traversing sparse 3D structures requires humanoid robots to perceive thin, overhanging geometry while executing agile, accurate whole-body motions. We study this problem through monkey-bar traversal, where the robot must jump to the structure, traverse it through sparse bar interactions, and land safely. For this task, we present a reinforcement-learning-based perceptive control system that operates directly on observations from a head-mounted solid-state lidar. To extract task-relevant geometry from the sparse returns, the policy consumes the raw lidar scan through an attention-based encoder with recurrent memory. This policy is obtained by a phase-scheduled teacher- student pipeline that combines privileged experts for jumping up, brachiating, and jumping down. For transfer to hardware, we model lidar noise, battery-voltage sag, and actuator thermal limits, and equip the humanoid with passive hook end-effectors for robust bar interaction. On hardware, the resulting policy completes the full jump-up->brachiation->jump-down sequence in 14 of 15 trials across three bar configurations and reaches brachiation speeds up to 0.5 m/s. Beyond brachiation, the same perception backbone supports a separately trained policy that ducks beneath thin overhead obstacles with 2 cm cross-sections.

</details>


### [300] [Sampling-based Certified Planning with Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29770)
*Peng Xie,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次量化了图凸集规划器（GCS）中区域生成器仅提供概率性无碰撞保证所带来的"间隙代价"：在14自由度双臂库上，3.2%的界面样本存在碰撞，却导致62%的查询答案错误。修复库不可行，作者转而构建了一个持续验证答案的规划器，通过采样重叠区域、剪枝、证书球链验证等机制，在全部29个任务查询上实现零无效答案，首次认证答案时间比参考方法快14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图凸集（GCS）规划器生成的轨迹虽然在构造上保证无碰撞，但前提是凸区域本身无碰撞——而区域生成器仅能概率性地保证这一性质，没有规划器在家族内验证它。作者希望测量这一概率保证与实际安全之间的差距究竟有多大成本，并探索解决方案。

Method: 作者首先测量了库中碰撞样本占比对搜索结果的影响，尝试了三种修复策略（更严格的验收标准、平方和认证区域、均匀边距）均失败。最终构建一个认证规划器：采样分解的重叠区域和共享面，用可采纳的有信息界剪枝，对每轮搜索提出的候选解用无分辨率参数的清除证书球链进行持续验证；失败时用局部区域内绕行修复，并重新验证凸优化结果。

Result: 在29个取放查询上与参考方法正面比较：认证规划器零无效答案（参考方法有21个），首次认证答案耗时0.11秒（参考方法未认证答案为1.59秒），并且在参考方法答案物理有效的每个查询上精确复现参考最优解。

Conclusion: 概率性区域保证与真实无碰撞之间的差距在GCS规划器中会造成严重的、不成比例的回答错误；与其修补区域生成器的概率保证，不如在规划器中构建持续认证机制来直接验证每个候选答案的安全性。

Abstract: Planners on graphs of convex sets return trajectories that are collision-free by construction, provided the convex regions are collision-free. The region generator only promises that property probabilistically, and no planner in the family verifies it. We report the first measurement of what the gap costs. On a scaled 14-DOF bimanual library, $3.2\%$ of interface samples are in collision, and a search-based GCS planner (\gcsstar) turns that volume error into a $62\%$ answer error: $18$ of $29$ pick-and-place queries return trajectories that drive the arms through the shelves, up to $91$\,mm deep, reported as successes. Repairing the library does not work; a ten times stricter acceptance contract, sums-of-squares certified regions, and uniform margins each destroy the connectivity planning needs before they deliver soundness. We instead build a planner that certifies its answers. It samples the overlaps and shared faces of the decomposition, prunes with an admissible informed bound, and verifies the one candidate each search round proposes, continuously, by a chain of clearance certificate balls with no resolution parameter; failures are repaired with local in-region detours, and the convex polish is re-verified. Head-to-head on all $29$ task queries it delivers zero invalid answers against $21$ for the reference, reaches its first certified answer in $0.11$\,s against $1.59$\,s for the reference's unverified one, and reproduces the reference optimum exactly on every query whose reference answer is physically valid.

</details>


### [301] [Self-Aware Active Learning Enables Continual Improvement in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29772)
*Dong Hu,Chao Huang,Carman K. M. Lee,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAGE（自感知引导探索）的主动学习框架，用于自动驾驶系统的训练后适应。该框架通过预测世界模型生成"恐惧"（短期预测风险与模型不确定性）和"好奇心"（预测误差衡量新颖性）两种在线内在信号，自适应地调节干预阈值，在风险超过阈值时将控制权转移给专家策略，并通过模仿学习将接管轨迹用于针对性改进。实验在多种模拟和真实场景中验证了该方法能提升鲁棒性、减少安全违规，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的自动驾驶系统在熟悉条件下表现可靠，但在罕见的分布偏移和长尾事件中容易发生突然失效。核心限制在于大多数智能体主要从被动经验中学习，缺乏估计自身能力不足的机制、无法及时寻求帮助，也无法将安全关键事件转化为有针对性的改进。

Method: SAGE框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 学习一个预测世界模型，生成"恐惧"信号（估计短期预测风险和模型不确定性）和"好奇心"信号（通过预测误差衡量新颖性）；(2) 好奇心自适应地校准"恐惧"的干预阈值，使智能体能够根据上下文动态调节风险容忍度；(3) 当预测恐惧超过自适应阈值时，将控制权转移给专家或回退策略，并利用接管轨迹进行聚焦模仿学习；同时将"恐惧"作为安全导向约束集成到策略优化和评估中，减少适应过程中的性能退化。

Result: 在模拟路线迁移任务、Waymo日志驾驶场景、CARLA遮挡危险场景以及真实移动机器人导航测试中，SAGE在novel和安全关键场景下提升了鲁棒性，减少了安全违规，同时保持了与强基线策略相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 智能体在初始训练后可以通过估计自身能力边界、在需要时请求指导、并从罕见高价值事件中选择性学习来持续改进。这为自动驾驶系统的后训练适应提供了一种新的思路。

Abstract: Learning-based autonomous driving (AD) systems can perform reliably in familiar conditions, yet rare distribution shifts and long-tail events remain a major source of abrupt failure. A central limitation is that most agents learn primarily from passive experience and lack mechanisms to estimate when their competence is insufficient, seek timely assistance, and convert safety-critical encounters into targeted improvement. Here we present self-aware guided exploration (SAGE), an active learning framework for post-training adaptation in AD. SAGE learns a predictive world model that generates two online intrinsic signals: fear, which estimates short-horizon predictive risk and model uncertainty, and curiosity, which measures novelty through prediction error. Curiosity adaptively calibrates the intervention threshold for fear, allowing the agent to regulate risk in a context-dependent manner. When predicted fear exceeds this adaptive threshold, the agent transfers control to an expert or fallback policy and uses the resulting takeover trajectories for focused imitation learning. In parallel, fear is integrated into policy optimization and evaluation as a safety-oriented constraint to reduce performance regressions during adaptation. We evaluate SAGE in simulated route-transfer tasks, Waymo-based logged driving scenarios, CARLA occlusion hazards, and real-world mobile robot navigation tests. Across these settings, SAGE improves robustness in novel and safety-critical scenarios, reduces safety violations, and maintains task performance comparable to strong baseline policies. These results suggest that agents can improve after initial training by estimating the limits of their competence, requesting guidance when needed, and learning selectively from rare high-value events.

</details>


### [302] [EMERGE-Policy: A Robot Mind Emerges Beyond a Single Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29896)
*Zhirui Fang,Qingchi Yu,Ziyang Chen,Longfei Li,Haoran Ma,Keru Zhou,Xinrun Xu,Samith Va,Yuxuan Hu,Peixuan Song,Qiang Du,Bin Qian,Yongkang Deng,Xin Li,Yezhen Wang,Zhe Li,Hao Luo,Shuyan Li,Ziwei Wang,Weijian Deng,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMERGE-Policy提出了一种图结构的多智能体智能体框架，通过主智能体与多个角色子智能体的协作分工（感知、执行监控、验证、记忆），在不额外微调的情况下提升了机器人策略在公开基准和真实机器人实验上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人有效的"心智"不必局限于单一策略，而是可以通过多个专业化组件在共享编排过程中的交互协作实现，作者希望探索超越孤立策略运行的系统级智能体框架。

Method: 构建图结构的多智能体框架：主智能体在活动上下文窗口中保留任务级状态，角色子智能体在隔离上下文中分别负责感知、执行监控、验证和记忆整合，返回结构化任务相关证据；通过功能性技能接口组合异构后端（操作、想象、评估技能）；采用基于标准的验证、文本故障诊断和分支栈恢复实现局部修正，并结合token感知的外部记忆保留任务状态。

Result: 无需额外微调即在多个具有广泛影响的公开基准上取得优秀表现，并完成了一系列真实机器人实验，表明该框架能超越孤立运行，实现鲁棒的机器人策略扩展。

Conclusion: 通过将不同功能子任务分配给多个智能体并发协作，以及将模型视为技能在框架内调用的技术范式，EMERGE-Policy证明了系统级策略能够从多智能体分工协作中获益，拓展了机器人策略的适用范围和鲁棒性。

Abstract: A robot's effective ``mind'' need not reside in a single policy. It can emerge when specialized components perceive, reason, predict, act, verify, and remember within a shared orchestration process. EMERGE-Policy turns this perspective into a graph-structured agentic framework that coordinates both capability invocation and information exchange. A Main Agent retains task-level state within an active context window, while role-specific Sub Agents process perception, execution monitoring, verification, and memory consolidation in isolated contexts and return structured, task-relevant evidence. Role-specific contexts control information load by exposing only decision-relevant evidence to the Main Agent, while the functional Skill interface composes heterogeneous backends as Operational, Imagination, and Evaluation Skills. Criterion-grounded verification, textual failure diagnosis, and Branch Stack recovery provide localized correction, with token-aware external memory preserving task-relevant state. Together, their closed-loop interaction realizes the system-level policy captured by the name EMERGE-Policy. Without additional fine-tuning, we achieved outstanding performance on several public benchmark that have had a wide-reaching impact, and conducted a series of real robot experiments. These system-level results suggest that through the division of different functional sub-tasks among multiple agents and their concurrent collaboration, as well as the technical paradigm where the model is regarded as a skill and called within the framework, EMERGE-Policy can extend the robust robot policies beyond isolated runs.

</details>


### [303] [System Identification of Admittance Models for Large Real-World Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29935)
*Nathan I. Baum,Nathaniel G. Luttmer,Mark A. Minor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个完整的物理一致模型生成工作流，用于从大型真实世界物体（如闭门器门和手推车）中自动估计物理参数，无需关节处的扭矩传感器，在保证惯性和摩擦参数物理一致性的同时，实现了较低的估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉接口仿真需要物理一致的动力模型，但现成的日常物体通常缺乏此类模型，限制了触觉仿真的保真度。现有方法难以处理具有各种约束和机构的大型真实物体，且常需要昂贵的传感器。

Method: 采用两阶段工作流：先分割物体并识别把手和主体，无需在铰链、轴等约束关节处安装扭矩传感器。门使用四杆机构运动学和基于流体动力学的集总参数模型（包括开启、回弹、摆动和闩锁区域）；手推车建模为具有球形轮且滚动无滑动的刚体。

Result: 两个物体的把手估计RMS误差分别低于0.64 N和0.042 Nm；门主体估计RMS误差为2.19 Nm，手推车主体估计RMS误差为6.77 Nm。

Conclusion: 该工作流能够为不同类型约束和不同复杂度的真实物体生成物理一致的模型，具有较好的估计精度，为高保真触觉仿真提供了可行的建模途径。

Abstract: Simulation of admittance-type models requires physically consistent dynamic models that are rarely available for off-the-shelf, everyday objects, limiting the fidelity of haptic interfaces that rely on such simulations. This paper presents the first complete workflow for producing physically consistent models of large real-world objects with various constraints and mechanisms, guaranteeing physical consistency of inertia and friction parameters. The workflow separates each object and identifies the handle and body in two stages, requiring no torque sensors at hinges, axles, or other constrained joints. Models are produced for a heavy, closer-actuated door and a wheelbarrow, representing objects of differing constraint types and model complexity. The door is modeled using four-bar linkage kinematics and a fluid dynamics-based lumped parameter model including opening, backcheck, swing, and latch zones. The wheelbarrow is modeled as a rigid body with a spherical wheel and no slip during rolling. Handle estimation RMS errors were below 0.64 N and 0.042 Nm across both objects. Door body estimation had RMS error of 2.19 Nm and wheelbarrow body estimation had RMS error of 6.77 Nm.

</details>


### [304] [Training-Free Action Correction for VLA Model Failures via Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29967)
*Owen Kwon,Pablo Ortega-Kral,Arthur Bucker,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了CorrectVLA框架，通过任务级自然语言修正转化为动作幅度调整，在不修改策略权重的情况下修复VLA模型在执行层面的失败，但对语义理解层面的失败无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在部署中表现出系统性失败，但这些失败发生的条件和能否在不重训练的情况下被修正仍不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: CorrectVLA框架将任务级自然语言修正转化为加性的动作幅度调整，不修改策略权重。由人类提供单一任务级修正，统一应用于所有rollouts，无需逐episode干预。

Result: 在仿真中恢复分布内和分布外任务的执行错位失败；在真实机器人（UFactory xArm7）环境偏移下恢复近完美成功率；通过LIBERO-90失败模式分类发现执行错位类失败可修正，语义理解类失败不可修正。

Conclusion: 该方法在策略具有战略正确性时成功，在缺乏基本语义理解时失败，为推理时修正建立了实际的操作边界。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate strong semantic understanding yet exhibit systematic failures during deployment. The conditions under which these failures occur, and whether they can be corrected without retraining, remain poorly understood. In this paper, we take steps toward addressing this gap. We present CorrectVLA, a framework that translates task-level natural language corrections into additive action magnitude adjustments without modifying policy weights. A human provides a single task-level correction, applied uniformly across all rollouts without per-episode intervention. In simulation, CorrectVLA recovers execution misalignment failures across both in-distribution and OOD tasks. In real-robot experiments on a UFactory xArm7 under environment shift, CorrectVLA restores near-perfect success where the base policy almost entirely breaks down, generalizing across object locations and identities. Through a taxonomy of failure modes on LIBERO-90, we find that execution misalignment failures, where the policy reaches the correct target but miscalibrates action magnitudes, represent the correctable subset, while other failure modes where semantic comprehension itself breaks down are not amenable to this approach. The approach succeeds when policies possess strategic correctness and fails when fundamental comprehension is absent, establishing a practical operational boundary for inference-time correction.

</details>


### [305] [MiBOT: A head-worn robot that modulates cardiovascular responses through human-like soft massage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30055)
*Alice Mylaeus,Stephanie Vogt,Berken Utku Demirel,Marcel Gort,Mirko Meboldt,Manuel Meier,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MiBOT，一种头戴式按摩机器人，利用气动人工肌肉驱动两个软触觉执行器模拟人类按摩动作，通过静音驱动设计，在受控实验中使受试者收缩压和舒张压分别降低2.8 mmHg和1.7 mmHg，心率平均降低8-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按摩机器人系统主要关注躯干和四肢的按摩治疗，但通过合适的驱动方式在人的头部执行按摩动作一直是一个挑战。

Method: MiBOT是一种头戴式按摩机器人，采用气动人工肌肉配合控制回路来响应接触压力，实现静音驱动，通过两个软触觉执行器产生模仿人类按摩的触摸动作。

Result: 在受控研究中，MiBOT产生了与人类按摩相似的积极定量结果：参与者平均收缩压和舒张压分别降低2.8 mmHg和1.7 mmHg，心率平均降低8-10%。

Conclusion: MiBOT的机械系统产生了积极且结论性的定量结果，其按摩效果与人工按摩相似，证明了头戴式按摩机器人的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Massage therapy is helpful for the rehabilitation of various diseases, such as headaches caused by migraines and stress. Existing robotic systems have focused on massage therapy on the torso and limbs, but performing massage motions through suitable actuation on a person's head has been a challenge. In this paper, we present MiBOT, a head-worn massage robot that actuates two soft tactors to produce touch motions mimicking human massage. A key design principle behind MiBOT is its silent actuation, which we achieve through pneumatic artificial muscles in conjunction with a controller loop to respond to contact pressure. We evaluated the effectiveness of MiBOT in a controlled study and assessed subjects' blood pressure and heart rate levels while applying MiBOT. We found that our mechanical system generated positive and conclusive quantitative outcomes that are similar to the human-administered massage, decreasing participants' mean systolic and diastolic blood pressure by 2.8 mmHg and 1.7 mmHg, respectively, as well as calming their heart rate by 8-10% on average.

</details>


### [306] [Rethinking Language's Role in Efficient VLA for Autonomous Vehicles: Toward Smarter, Trustworthy Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30144)
*Tongfei Guo,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言残差"分类法（L1-L4），系统梳理了VLA模型在自动驾驶推理阶段中语言的使用时机与位置，并从延迟、参数、内存、FLOPs、令牌数五个部署维度对代表性方法进行分析，探讨了NLP/LLM高效方法在AD中的适配与约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在自动驾驶中展现出巨大潜力，但语言推理在车端代价高昂——延迟和内存预算紧张，且自回归解码本质上是串行的。训练成本仅支付一次，而推理成本在每一帧部署中都会重复产生，因此亟需重新审视"何时、何地"使用语言进行推理这一核心问题。

Method: 作者提出Language Residue分类法，将推理时语言使用方式分为四类：L1（仅训练时监督）、L2（潜在非文本推理）、L3（条件触发调用）、L4（全帧生成）。对代表性方法进行标注，并在五个部署轴（延迟、参数、内存、FLOPs、令牌数）上分析，同时追踪NLP/LLM中的高效方法如何在AD中适配。

Result: 在nuScenes、NAVSIM、Bench2Drive等主流开环与闭环驾驶基准上对代表方法进行了系统分析，揭示了不同语言使用层级在部署约束下的权衡，并识别了NLP/LLM高效方法向AD迁移的动因与制约因素。

Conclusion: 语言并非在所有推理阶段都应被使用，按需、分层地控制语言残差是实现在地部署VLA模型的关键方向；该领域仍需在效率与语义能力之间寻找更优平衡。相关方法将持续在GitHub上更新维护。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are reshaping autonomous driving (AD) by unifying perception, reasoning, and control through language, enabling semantic grounding, interpretable decisions, and better long-tail generalization. But language is expensive onboard: latency and memory budgets are tight, and autoregressive decoding is inherently sequential. This work reframes the central question as when and where language should act at inference, since inference cost recurs at every deployed frame while training cost is paid once. We introduce the Language Residue taxonomy to organize methods by their inference-time use of language: train-time-only supervision (L1), latent non-textual reasoning (L2), conditional invocation (L3), and full per-frame generation (L4). We review representative methods and tag each across five deployment axes (latency, parameters, memory, FLOPs, tokens), analyzing them on major open- and closed-loop driving benchmarks (e.g., nuScenes, NAVSIM, Bench2Drive). We further trace how efficient methods from NLP/LLM are adapted in AD, identifying the constraints and motivations driving these adaptations. A continuously updated repository will be available at Github.

</details>


### [307] [Contrast-Free Autonomous Navigation of Untethered Endovascular Microrobots Using Single-Plane Fluoroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30220)
*Husnu Halid Alabay,Tuan-Anh Le,Ping Wang,Hakan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA（Virtual Integration for Spatial Tracking and Autonomy）框架，一种数字孪生驱动的自主导航系统，使磁性驱动的无束缚微型机器人在单平面X射线透视下无需造影剂即可进行3D血管内导航。通过将3D血管重建为数字孪生、离散化中心线为里程碑并分配2D机器人位置到最近投影里程碑，VISTA将单平面透视观测转换为拓扑约束的导航状态。在多个血管模型和活体大鼠下腔静脉中验证，相比传统透视人工控制，导航时间减少62%，校正指令减少98%，辐射暴露减少57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性驱动无束缚微型机器人的可靠3D导航是临床转化的主要障碍。单平面X射线透视仅提供2D投影，缺乏深度信息，而双平面成像或反复造影剂增强血管造影会增加手术复杂性、辐射暴露或造影剂负担。现有方法无法在无造影剂条件下实现自主3D导航，亟需一种利用广泛可用的单平面透视设备的解决方案。

Method: VISTA框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将血管解剖结构重建为3D数字孪生；(2) 将血管中心线离散化为导航里程碑（milestones）；(3) 将检测到的2D机器人位置分配给最近的投影里程碑，相邻里程碑定义局部血管方向并据此生成磁场驱动指令。该方法将单平面透视观测转化为拓扑约束的导航状态，全程无需造影剂。

Result: VISTA在解剖结构不同的血管模型（连续流动条件下）和活体大鼠下腔静脉中均成功演示。与传统透视人机环控制相比：导航时间减少高达62%，校正驱动指令减少高达98%，辐射暴露减少高达57%。

Conclusion: VISTA确立了一种数字孪生引导的无造影剂自主导航框架，可在广泛可用的单平面透视设备下实现无束缚血管内微型机器人的自主导航，为临床转化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable three-dimensional (3D) navigation of magnetically actuated untethered microrobots remains a major barrier to clinical translation. X-ray fluoroscopy is the standard real-time imaging modality for endovascular procedures, but single-plane fluoroscopy provides only a two-dimensional (2D) projection, eliminating depth information and complicating autonomous navigation. Recovering this information through biplane imaging or repeated contrast-enhanced angiography increases procedural complexity, radiation exposure, or contrast burden. Here, we introduce VISTA (Virtual Integration for Spatial Tracking and Autonomy), a digital twin framework enabling contrast-free autonomous navigation under single-plane fluoroscopy. VISTA reconstructs vascular anatomy as a 3D digital twin, discretizes the vessel centerline into navigation milestones, and assigns the detected 2D robot position to the nearest projected milestone. Consecutive milestones define the local vessel orientation used to generate magnetic actuation commands, converting single-plane fluoroscopic observations into topology-constrained navigation states without requiring contrast injection during navigation. VISTA is demonstrated across anatomically distinct vascular phantoms under continuous flow and within the inferior vena cava of a live rat in vivo. Compared with conventional fluoroscopic human-in-the-loop control, VISTA reduced navigation time by up to 62%, corrective actuation commands by up to 98%, and radiation exposure by up to 57%. These results establish VISTA as a digital twin-guided framework for contrast-free autonomous navigation of untethered endovascular microrobots using widely available single-plane fluoroscopy.

</details>


### [308] [Optimized Modular Design and Development of a Tilt-Rotor Bicopter Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30222)
*Saideep Verma,Nimisha Tatapudi,Akshay Arjun,Danwada Sharanya Sukka,Abhishek Sarkar,Joyjit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对倾转旋翼双旋翼无人机进行了模块化设计与验证，通过结构分析、气动仿真、重心与中性点位置控制以及推力动力分析，确保飞行器在不同飞行模式下的静动态稳定性，并通过实际飞行测试验证了仿真性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾转旋翼双旋翼无人机兼具固定翼长航时与旋翼垂直起降的优势，但需要优化设计以确保静动态稳定性。传统双旋翼无人机在结构设计、重心与中性点位置控制等方面缺乏系统性分析与验证。

Method: 采用模块化设计方法，结合结构分析（验证机身承受不同飞行模式应力）、气动仿真（控制重心CG与中性点NP的相对位置保证悬停和前飞稳定性）、推力与动力分析（评估飞行性能和续航），最终开发样机并使用基础飞行控制器进行实际飞行测试。

Result: 完成了双旋翼无人机的模块化开发，通过仿真与飞行测试对比验证，确认了结构强度、稳定性控制策略及飞行性能指标的可行性与准确性。

Conclusion: 该模块化设计方法有效保证了倾转旋翼双旋翼无人机在悬停和前飞模式下的静动态稳定性，仿真结果与实际飞行测试指标吻合，验证了设计流程的可靠性。

Abstract: Hybrid systems like tilt-rotor bicopter drones combine the beneficial characteristics of both fixed-wing and rotary-wing technology, enabling long endurance and VTOL capability. However, such drones also require an optimum design to ensure both static and dynamic stability. The modular design of a traditional bicopter is developed in this paper based on extensive analysis and in-depth structural and aerodynamic simulations. The structural analysis has been performed to ensure that the aircraft's structure withstands the stresses encountered during different flight modes. Controlling the relative positions of the Center of Gravity (CG) and Neutral Point (NP) is an essential aspect of the design, ensuring stability during hover and positive stability during forward flight. The thrust and power analyses have been conducted to assess the flight performance and endurance. After analysis, the drone has been developed, and flight tests with a basic flight controller were conducted to validate the performance metrics obtained in the simulation.

</details>


### [309] [Motus2: A Self-Evolving General World Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30237)
*Hongzhe Bi,Zihao Zhou,Yihang Tang,Jingrui Pang,Shuhe Huang,Haitian Liu,Runqing Wang,Shuai Huang,Yichen Wang,Yiming Cheng,Ruowen Zhao,Zhenghua Li,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Jinhui Wan,Jiabao Liu,Min Zhao,Fan Bao,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Motus2是一个自进化的通用世界模型，用于灵巧操作任务。它通过单一共享权重的模型同时暴露策略（世界-动作模型）、模拟器（动作条件世界模型）和评估器（价值模型）三个控制接口，三者耦合形成闭环的决策-学习循环。通过模型扩展和数据扩展（从单目到立体仿生数据再到机器人域适应）实现自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用具身智能体的世界模型通常只是在世界模拟器上附加动作输出头，没有将决策和学习耦合为闭环的改进循环。作者希望构建一个真正将感知、预测、行动、评估和改进统一在一个系统中的世界模型。

Method: 1) 模型扩展：单一共享权重模型同时提供策略、模拟器、评估器三个接口，策略提出候选动作块，模拟器预测视觉后果，评估器评估预测结果，形成闭环决策-学习循环。2) 数据扩展：从大规模单目第一人称数据到同步立体第一人称数据，再到机器人域适应，数据规模逐步升级。3) 进一步研究全局自回归和混合记忆架构扩展滑动窗口上下文，加入触觉反馈，并在完整的仿生平台上实例化（立体视觉、双臂、双手、触觉感知）。

Result: 论文展示了Motus2在模型扩展和数据扩展两方面推进世界建模。通过优秀的专家演示用于动作学习，失败和次优交互用于动力学建模和价值学习，在仿生平台上实现了灵巧操作的自我进化能力。

Conclusion: Motus2提出了一种通用路径：通过数据扩展和闭环通用世界模型扩展相结合，实现自我进化的灵巧操作。这种设计使得智能体能够在统一的系统中不断改进自身能力。

Abstract: General embodied agents should perceive, predict, act, evaluate, and improve within a unified system. World models have shown great promise in building such agents, yet existing models typically append an action output head to a world simulator, without coupling them into a closed decision-and-learning loop for policy improvement. We present Motus2, a self-evolving general world model for dexterous manipulation. Motus2 advances world modeling through model scaling and data scaling. For model scaling, a single model with shared weights exposes three control interfaces: a policy (world-action model), a simulator (action-conditioned world model), and an evaluator (value model). The policy proposes candidate action chunks, the simulator predicts their visual consequences, and the evaluator assesses the predicted outcomes. Their coupling forms a closed decision-and-learning loop for policy improvement. This formulation uses curated expert demonstrations for action learning, while failed and suboptimal interactions provide valuable evidence for dynamics modeling and value learning. For data scaling, Motus2 progresses from large-scale monocular egocentric data to synchronized stereo egocentric data, followed by robot-domain adaptation with robot trajectories and supplementary human-robot alignment data. Motus2 further studies global-autoregressive and hybrid-memory extensions of its sliding-window context, adds tactile feedback for contact-aware control, and is instantiated on a fully biomimetic platform with stereo vision, dual arms, dual dexterous hands, and tactile sensing. Together, egocentric data scaling and closed-loop general world model scaling provide a general path toward self-evolving dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [310] [CanonNav: Disentangling Navigation Behavior from Camera Geometry in Cross-Platform Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30242)
*Dong-Wook Kim,Ji-Hoon Hwang,E-In Son,Mintaek Oh,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CanonNav是一个视觉导航框架，通过相机几何标准化（camera geometry canonicalization）将视觉观测与轨迹转换到相机一致的表示空间，并利用离线可通行性估计器的伪标签提供安全性和局部进度监督，从而解耦导航行为与相机几何，提升跨平台示范学习的效果。实验表明该方法在仅使用RGB输入的条件下优于RGB基线方法，甚至在困难场景中超越RGB-D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台示范的模仿学习在视觉导航中存在两个核心问题：一是直接从图像-轨迹对学习会将导航行为与平台相关的相机几何纠缠在一起，迫使策略从视觉观测中隐式推断相机几何，这是一个病态问题；二是模仿学习只捕捉专家的动作选择，而忽略了运动背后的中间决策过程。这两个问题阻碍了跨平台示范数据的一致学习。

Method: CanonNav提出两个关键机制：（1）相机几何标准化（camera geometry canonicalization），将视觉观测和轨迹转换到相机一致的表示空间，解耦导航行为与相机几何；（2）通过离线可通行性估计器生成伪标签，提供安全性和局部进度监督，安全监督惩罚不安全的轨迹，局部进度监督指导机器人前进方向，将规划监督信号补充到模仿学习过程中。

Result: 在多种相机配置和环境的实验中，CanonNav在仅使用RGB推理的条件下一致地优于基于RGB的基线方法，并且在具有挑战性的场景中超越了基于RGB-D的方法。

Conclusion: CanonNav通过解耦导航行为与相机几何并引入互补的规划监督，有效提升了跨平台视觉导航的泛化能力，证明了即使仅使用RGB输入也能在困难场景中达到甚至超越RGB-D方法的性能。

Abstract: While visual navigation has advanced through imitation learning from cross-platform demonstrations, fully leveraging such data remains challenging. First, directly learning from image-trajectory pairs entangles navigation behavior with platform-dependent camera geometry. This hinders consistent learning by forcing the policy to implicitly infer camera geometry from visual observations, an inherently ill-posed problem. Second, imitation learning from demonstrated trajectories captures the expert's chosen motion but leaves the intermediate decisions underlying that motion implicit. To address these issues, we propose CanonNav, a visual navigation framework that disentangles navigation behavior from camera geometry and incorporates complementary planning supervision into learning from cross-platform demonstrations. CanonNav introduces camera geometry canonicalization, which transforms visual observations and trajectories into a camera-consistent representation space. Building on this representation, we derive safety and local-progress supervision using pseudo-labels from an offline traversability estimator. Safety supervision penalizes unsafe trajectories, while local-progress supervision guides where the robot should advance. Experiments across diverse camera configurations and environments show that, despite using only RGB at inference, CanonNav consistently outperforms RGB-based baselines and even surpasses RGB-D-based methods in challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [311] [CometVLA: Co-Training on an Embodied Data Pyramid towards Physical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30289)
*Hanwen Wan,Dafeng Chi,Linbo Zhai,Tianao Shen,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Peidong Liu,Liang Lin,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CometVLA框架，通过构建与机器人动作数据严格对齐的实体物理VQA语料库（CometData）和基准（CometBench），引入全局动作先验（GAP）令牌作为可学习的瓶颈，将物理常识注入动作头而不破坏预训练VLM骨干。通过跨遥操作、仿真、第一视角轨迹和VQA层级的联合训练，在真实操作任务和RoboTwin仿真中优于强VLA基线，并证明物理理解预训练确实能提升下游操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在需要物理常识的操作任务中表现脆弱，主要因为：1）当前的物理VQA数据通常是"无实体的"（disembodied），与机器人动作域不对齐；2）第一视角视频仅作为辅助预训练使用；3）尚不明确改进VLM的物理理解是否真的有利于下游动作生成。因此需要一种将物理理解与机器人动作域严格对齐的方法。

Method: 1）构建CometData和CometBench，一个与机器人动作数据和体态严格对齐的实体物理VQA语料库和基准；2）引入全局动作先验（GAP）令牌，这是一种紧凑的可学习瓶颈，用于隔离任务无关的运动规律，使动作头能够消费物理常识而不会破坏预训练的VLM骨干；3）在实体数据金字塔上进行联合训练，涵盖遥操作、仿真、第一视角轨迹和VQA层级。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务和RoboTwin仿真中，CometVLA一致优于强VLA基线。相关性分析表明，在CometBench上更强的VLM性能预示着更高的VLA成功率，验证了物理理解预训练确实能让下游操作任务受益。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了物理理解预训练对下游操作任务有真实的积极影响，通过将物理VQA数据与机器人动作域对齐并设计GAP令牌的隔离机制，有效提升了VLA模型在需要物理常识的操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models remain brittle in manipulation tasks that require physical commonsense. Current physical VQA data is typically disembodied and misaligned with robot action domains. Egocentric videos are used only as auxiliary pre-training. It remains unclear whether improved VLM physical understanding actually benefits downstream action generation. Therefore, we present CometVLA to close this gap. We construct CometData and CometBench, an embodied physical VQA corpus and benchmark strictly aligned with the robot's action data and embodiment. We introduce Global Action Prior (GAP) tokens, a compact learnable bottleneck that isolates task-agnostic motion regularities and lets the action head consume physical commonsense without corrupting the pre-trained VLM backbone. We co-train CometVLA across the embodied data pyramid, spanning teleoperation, simulation, egocentric trajectories, and VQA layers. On real-world manipulation tasks and RoboTwin simulation, CometVLA consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines. Correlation analysis shows that stronger VLM performance on CometBench indicates higher VLA success rates. Results demonstrate that physical understanding pre-training genuinely benefits downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [312] [Data-Centric Neuromotor Interfaces for Portable Human-Machine Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30301)
*Jiaxuan Li,Di Wu,Jianhua Liu,Yuxin Zhao,Jinnuo Li,Xiao Zhang,Zhenzhi Ying,Changsheng Dai,Xiang Li,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以数据为中心的神经运动接口范式，通过高带宽无线系统采集肌电信号，利用生理特征的可分离性，仅用2,210个参数的轻量模型即可在34个手势上实现94.36%的识别准确率，为边缘设备上的灵巧解码建立了千参数级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧的人机交互需要直观且可高效部署在资源受限边缘设备上的接口。虽然柔性电子皮肤可实现高保真数据采集，但实际部署中计算资源与便携性之间存在权衡，现有方法难以在轻量模型与高识别精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用数据中心的范式，通过无线高带宽系统与肌肉特异性电极集成为表面肌电接口，采集多种电生理信号。核心思想是通过生理特征获取基本可分离性，为识别提供足够的判别信息，从而允许使用极轻量的模型进行手势解码。

Result: 仅含2,210个参数的模型在34个手势上达到94.36%的准确率，可在边缘设备上快速部署，建立了千参数级灵巧解码的新基准。研究还阐明了数据中心范式中数据与算法之间的交互机制，验证了其在实际场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究为可靠神经运动接口的实际部署提供了一条有原则且经过验证的路径，证明了以数据为中心的思路在资源受限条件下实现高精度灵巧交互的可能性。

Abstract: Dexterous human-machine interaction requires intuitive and expressive interfaces that can be efficiently deployed on constrained edge devices. Flexible material-based neuromotor interfaces hold considerable promise, as they decode human movement intention into natural control. Although emerging flexible electronic skins enable wearable high-fidelity data acquisition, practical deployment inevitably involves trade-offs between computational resources and portability. We present a data-centric paradigm where physiological features yield fundamental separability, providing sufficient discriminative cues for recognition. A wireless, high-bandwidth system developed for collecting various electrophysiological signals, when integrated with muscle-specific electrodes, forms a surface electromyography-based interface. Exploiting highly separable data, a 2,210-parameter model achieves 94.36% accuracy across 34 gestures and can be rapidly deployed on edge devices, establishing a new thousand-parameter benchmark for dexterous decoding. The underlying data-algorithm interactions in the data-centric paradigm are further clarified, demonstrating its feasibility in real-world scenarios. This study provides a principled and validated pathway for practical deployment of reliable neuromotor interfaces.

</details>


### [313] [SpectraTac: A Compact Camera-Free Optical Tactile Sensor with Distributed Color Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30368)
*Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Yu Feng,Yutong Wang,Yanzhe Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpectraTac，一种紧凑、无相机、低成本（低于5美元）的光学触觉传感器，通过主动RGB照明和分布式颜色传感实现三维力估计与接触区域识别，力预测MAE低至0.161-0.429 N，九区域接触分类准确率达99.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感对机器人和人机交互至关重要，但现有方案难以同时兼顾丰富的触觉信息、紧凑的硬件尺寸、低成本与低计算开销，传统基于相机的方案存在体积大、成本高和图像处理开销大的问题。

Method: 采用无相机的光学方案：接触使柔性透明弹性体形变并调制内部光场，结合主动RGB照明和三个分布式颜色传感器捕获低维空间-光谱特征；通过数据驱动的解码框架同时估计三维力和接触区域。

Result: 三维力预测在x、y、z轴的MAE分别为0.161、0.164和0.429 N；九区域接触分类准确率99.9%；在实时三维力跟踪和基于接触区域的人机交互控制任务中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 分布式颜色分辨光学传感可作为基于相机的触觉传感的紧凑、低成本替代方案，适用于机器人、可穿戴感知和交互系统。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for physical interaction in robotics and human--machine systems. However, combining rich tactile information with compact hardware, low cost, and low computational overhead remains challenging. This work presents SpectraTac, a compact, camera-free optical tactile sensor that combines active red--green--blue (RGB) illumination with spatially distributed color sensing. Contact deforms a compliant transparent elastomer and modulates its internal light field, producing spatially differentiated changes in color and intensity. Three distributed color sensors capture these responses as low-dimensional spatio-spectral features, avoiding cameras, imaging optics, and high-dimensional image processing. The device measures 19.2 mm in diameter and 4 mm in height, with a material cost below USD~5. A data-driven decoding framework simultaneously estimates three-dimensional (3D) force and the contact region from the optical measurements. For 3D force prediction, the sensor achieved mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 0.161, 0.164, and 0.429 N along the x-, y-, and z-axes, respectively. The nine-region contact-classification accuracy was 99.9%. We further evaluated real-time 3D force tracking and contact-region-based human--machine interaction through an interactive control task. These results indicate that distributed color-resolved optical sensing offers a compact, low-cost alternative to camera-based tactile sensing for robotics, wearable sensing, and interactive systems.

</details>


### [314] [PAVE: Predictive Alignment and Value-Guided Evolution for World-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30378)
*Botong Zhao,Fang Yu,Tim,Senhua Zhu,Xinyuan Chen,Yue Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接世界-动作策略（\method），将结果无关的预测学习与结果感知的策略改进相结合。通过多时间尺度轨迹对齐进行训练，并使用独立的分位数价值评论者计算N步优势并转化为正/负/空文本条件来指导流匹配动作器，在三个仿真基准上取得了最强综合性能，同时保持了直接动作生成的在线执行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉-语言-动作策略虽然能高效生成连续机器人动作，但标准行为克隆存在两个互补的缺陷：表征没有被明确要求描述多时间尺度下的场景演化，且不同质量的部署轨迹常被不加区分地复用，未分离有效动力学与不良行为。

Method: \method 包含两个核心组件：(1) 保留局部固定偏移JEPA目标，并添加轨迹相对的多视界转换对齐（发生在剩余回合的25%、50%、75%和100%处），仅作为训练目标以保持局部物理变化和长程任务进展的表征；(2) 训练独立的分布性价值评论者，计算与动作块对齐的N步优势，并将其转换为正、负或空文本条件，用于流匹配动作器。在线执行时移除多视界预测器和评论者，保留从当前观测、语言指令和本体感觉直接生成动作的路径。

Result: 在三个仿真基准上，\method 取得了最强的综合性能，同时保持了直接动作器的在线执行路径不变。

Conclusion: 通过将多时间尺度的预测学习与条件化的策略改进相结合，\method 使得每条有效轨迹都能教授物理发生了什么，而动作器仅在相对更优行为的条件下部署，实现了训练阶段的信息充分利用与推理阶段的高效直接执行。

Abstract: Direct vision-language-action policies generate continuous robot actions efficiently, but standard behavior cloning leaves two complementary gaps: their representations are not explicitly required to describe how the scene evolves over multiple time scales, and deployment trajectories of unequal quality are often reused without separating useful dynamics from undesirable behavior. We introduce \method, a direct world-action policy that combines outcome-agnostic predictive learning with outcome-aware policy improvement. \method first retains a local fixed-offset JEPA objective and adds trajectory-relative multi-horizon transition alignment at 25%, 50%, 75%, and 100% of the remaining episode. These training-only targets require the current policy representation to preserve both local physical changes and longer-range task progress, without supplying explicit future tokens to the action head. \method then trains an independent distributional value critic on cumulative deployment trajectories, computes action-chunk-aligned $N$-step advantages, and converts them into positive, negative, or null text conditions for a flow-matching actor. Thus, every valid trajectory can teach what physically happened, while the actor is deployed only under the condition associated with relatively better actions. The multi-horizon predictor and critic are removed from online execution, preserving direct action generation from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioception. \redclaim{Across the three simulation benchmarks, \method achieves the strongest overall performance while preserving the direct actor's online execution path.}

</details>


### [315] [A Hybrid PEM-GP Framework for Uncertainty-Aware System Identification of Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30433)
*Abdallah Ghoul,Ismail Khalil Bousserhane,Kadri Boufeldja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理建模与数据驱动学习的四旋翼动力学建模框架，先使用预测误差方法（PEM）识别物理模型以捕捉系统主要结构，再用高斯过程（GP）学习残差动态。在Duckiedrone实验平台上验证，该方法在预测精度上与LSTM网络相当，同时还能提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统辨识方法虽具有可解释性，但难以捕捉小型四旋翼平台中显著的非线性效应；而纯数据驱动方法虽能有效表示复杂非线性动力学，却缺乏可解释性和良好校准的不确定性估计。这促使作者寻求一种兼顾两者优点的建模框架。

Method: 提出PEM-GP混合框架：首先使用预测误差方法（PEM）辨识一个基于物理的模型来捕捉系统主要结构，然后利用高斯过程（GP）对剩余动力学（残差）进行建模，从而能够区分已知物理效应与未建模动态。

Result: 在类Duckiedrone的实验平台上验证，PEM-GP模型的预测精度与长短期记忆（LSTM）网络相当，同时还额外提供了校准的不确定性估计，兼顾了精度与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该混合建模框架有效结合了物理模型的可解释性与数据驱动方法的表达能力，改善了模型可靠性，并支持不确定性感知的决策，为四旋翼控制中的动力学建模提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Accurate dynamic models play a central role in achieving reliable control of quadcopters. Classical system identification methods remain widely used, mainly because of their interpretability. However, they often fail to capture important nonlinear effects, especially in small-scale aerial platforms where such effects become more pronounced.
  Data-driven approaches offer a different perspective. They can represent complex nonlinear dynamics more effectively, but this comes at the cost of reduced interpretability and the absence of well-calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  In this work, we propose a framework that combines physics-based modeling with data-driven learning, while explicitly accounting for uncertainty. A physics-based model is first identified using the Prediction Error Method (PEM), which captures the main structure of the system. The remaining dynamics are then modeled using a Gaussian Process (GP), allowing the residual behavior to be learned directly from data. This separation makes it possible to distinguish between known physical effects and unmodeled dynamics.
  The proposed framework is validated on a Duckiedrone-like experimental setup. The results show that the PEM-GP model achieves prediction accuracy comparable to that of a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network, while additionally providing calibrated uncertainty estimates. This combination improves model reliability and supports uncertainty-aware decision-making.

</details>


### [316] [Anomaly Detection on Small Industrial Components via Vision-Based Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30506)
*G. F. Preziosa,M. Casiglia,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于视觉的触觉传感器（GelSight Mini）在工业异常检测中的应用，通过四种特征嵌入方法在五个真实工业组件上的对比实验，提出了三种面向部署约束的验证方案，为工业质检提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型工业零件的缺陷由几何特征驱动，普通光学相机难以捕捉亚厘米级零件的精细表面几何信息。视觉触觉传感器虽能将接触印记转换为高分辨率图像数据，但其在工业异常检测中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用安装在协作机器人上的GelSight Mini传感器采集五个真实工业组件的触觉数据集，对SPADE、PaDiM、FAPM和InReaCh四种特征嵌入方法进行无监督异常检测比较，并设置了三种面向部署的验证方案：Good Fraction分析（确定最少名义接触次数）、跨位置评估（测试不同接触位置的泛化能力）、低分辨率与高分辨率对比（评估成本效益）。

Result: 系统性地完成了四种方法在真实触觉数据集上的基准测试，通过三种部署约束驱动的验证方案得出了各类方法在不同场景下的性能表现，并验证了视觉触觉传感作为工业质检替代方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 视觉触觉传感器可成为工业质量控制的有效替代方案，研究结果为在工业异常检测中采用触觉传感的研究者和从业者提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Automated inspection of small industrial components, including sub-centimetre-scale parts where defects are geometry-driven and poorly resolved by standard optical cameras, calls for sensing modalities that can directly capture fine surface geometry. Vision-based tactile sensors address this need by converting contact imprints into high-resolution image-like data compatible with existing deep-learning pipelines, yet their effective use for industrial anomaly detection (AD) remains largely unexplored. This work systematically evaluates unsupervised AD methods on a real tactile dataset covering five genuine industrial components acquired with a GelSight Mini sensor mounted on a collaborative robot. Four feature-embedding methods, SPADE, PaDiM, FAPM, and InReaCh, are compared under three validations explicitly motivated by the deployment constraints of contact-based sensing: a Good Fraction analysis establishing the minimum number of nominal contacts for stable performance, directly bounded by gel wear since every acquisition degrades the soft interface; a cross-position evaluation assessing generalization across different contact locations observing the same recurring surface pattern; and a low- versus high-resolution comparison evaluating the cost-benefit of higher-resolution tactile acquisition. Overall, this systematic benchmarking study provides practical guidance for researchers and practitioners adopting vision-based tactile sensing for industrial AD and shows how this modality can serve as a viable alternative for industrial quality-control tasks.

</details>


### [317] [Behavior-Skill: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30536)
*Chunyun Ma,Lun Luo,Xingjian Luo,Xiexing Feng,Hang Zhang,Wei Liu,Feng Qiao,Yaonan Wang,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Behavior-Skill基准，将长时程移动操作任务重构为可执行的技能单元进行学习与评估。该基准包含50个家庭任务、34个技能类别、235,492个技能实例，每个实例配备技能指令、对齐的观测-动作片段、可恢复的中间状态和技能成功条件。实验表明不同技能间的失败分布极不均匀，接触丰富的操作技能是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖完整任务回放和任务级聚合指标，难以观察和分析中间阶段的失败，无法揭示长时程任务中具体技能层面的能力短板。

Method: 将长时程任务重构为可执行的构成性技能，创建包含23.5万+技能实例的基准；每个实例配对技能指令与观测-动作片段，并关联可恢复的中间状态和技能成功条件以支持独立评估；同时引入轨迹级和技能级指标来刻画策略能力。

Result: 在完整的50任务基准上对pi0.5和GR00T等代表性VLA策略进行实验，发现不同技能的失败率高度不均匀，接触丰富的操作技能（contact-rich manipulation skills）构成持续瓶颈。

Conclusion: Behavior-Skill通过暴露中间能力画像补充了全任务评估的不足，为分析和改进长时程VLA策略提供了有效工具，基准已公开。

Abstract: Reliable execution of long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks remains challenging because overall task success depends on the successful completion of multiple constituent skills. Existing benchmarks, however, still rely primarily on full-task rollouts and aggregate task-level metrics, making intermediate failures difficult to observe and analyze. We present Behavior-Skill, a benchmark that reformulates the learning and evaluation of long-horizon tasks around executable constituent skills. It contains 235,492 skill instances from 10,000 demonstrations across 50 household tasks and 34 semantic skill categories. Each instance pairs a skill instruction with an aligned observation-action segment, and is further associated with a restorable intermediate state and a skill success condition to enable independent evaluation under valid preconditions. We further introduce trajectory-level and skill-level metrics to characterize policy capability beyond aggregate task success. Extensive experiments across representative VLA policies including pi0.5 and GR00T on the complete 50-task benchmark show that failures are highly non-uniform across skills, with contact-rich manipulation skills forming persistent bottlenecks. These results demonstrate that Behavior-Skill complements full-task evaluation by exposing intermediate capability profiles for analyzing and improving long-horizon VLA policies. Behavior-Skill is publicly available at https://github.com/nubot-nudt/Behavior-Skill.

</details>


### [318] [Temporal Forcing: 4D Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30643)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yinghuan Shi,Chaoyang Zhao,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Forcing方法，通过引入历史路径并利用预训练4D基础模型的几何特征进行对齐，使视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型能够理解场景随时间的演变，解决长时程操作任务中因缺乏时间信息导致的观测混淆问题。在LIBERO基准上达到98.8%的成功率，在物理遮挡放置任务中将成功率从20.0%提升至43.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法通过与3D场景几何对齐来提升操作性能，但仅捕捉当前状态，缺乏时间维度信息，导致在长时程操作和视觉相似状态观测混淆（observation aliasing）问题上表现不佳。

Method: 首先引入历史路径（history pathway），使基础VLA模型能够将观测历史总结为时间感知的潜在表示；然后将这些潜在表示与预训练4D基础模型提取的几何特征进行对齐，该模型通过时间一致的几何表示捕捉动态三维世界的演变过程。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到98.8%的成功率，比基础模型高出2.2个百分点；在物理遮挡放置任务中，完整任务成功率从20.0%提升至43.3%。

Conclusion: Temporal Forcing通过4D表示对齐为VLA模型补充了时间信息，有效提升了长时程操作和动态环境理解能力，实验证明该方法在模拟和真实物理任务中均有显著性能提升，代码将公开。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) methods improve manipulation performance by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods often struggle with long-horizon manipulation and observation aliasing between visually similar states due to a lack of temporal information: the 3D scene geometry captures only the current state, rather than how it has evolved over time. To resolve this, we present Temporal Forcing, a 4D representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, we first introduce a history pathway that enables a vanilla VLA model to summarize observation history into temporally aware latent representations. Then, the latent representations are aligned with the geometric features extracted by a pretrained 4D foundation model, which captures the evolving 3D world through temporally consistent geometric representations, enabling a deeper understanding of dynamic environments. Temporal Forcing reaches 98.8% on LIBERO, outperforming its base model by 2.2 points. On a physical hidden-placement task, it raises full-task success from 20.0% to 43.3%. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [319] [CIG-RL: Curiosity-Driven Information-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Source Term Estimation in Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30673)
*Junhee Lee,Seunghwan Kim,Hongro Jang,Hyungjin Kim,Hyoungho Park,Changseung Kim,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习方法（CIRL），用于在噪声环境下进行高效鲁棒的源项估计（STE）。该方法通过主动探索训练中未充分探索的新颖信念状态转移，并引入不确定性自适应的主动感知奖励，在高噪声仿真和真实实验中验证了其鲁棒性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度强化学习（DRL）的源项估计需要在噪声环境中根据信念状态选择动作，但现有方法缺乏有效的探索策略（依赖随机探索或仅基于信念不确定性减少），导致策略在噪声环境下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习框架：1）通过好奇心机制鼓励智能体主动探索训练中未曾充分探索的新颖信念状态转移；2）引入不确定性自适应的主动感知奖励，引导在不确定性条件下的高效源搜索。

Result: 在高温噪声条件下的仿真实验以及真实世界实验中，该方法均验证了其鲁棒性和可行性，展示了在实际STE问题中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的好奇心驱动信息引导强化学习方法能够有效提升DRL在噪声环境下的源项估计性能，为实际有害气体泄漏识别任务提供了稳健且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Source term estimation (STE), which aims to estimate key properties of the gas source, is essential for identifying hazardous gas releases. Information-theoretic approaches have been adopted for autonomous STE using mobile sensors due to robustness in noisy environments, yet their online action selection incurs substantial computational cost. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising alternative with its fast decision-making capability. In DRL-based STE, the agent selects actions based on belief states of the source term updated from noisy measurement sequences. However, existing methods rely on random exploration or solely on belief uncertainty reduction without an effective exploration strategy in DRL, which can limit policy robustness in noisy environments.
